# DL4J Full Workflow

All-in-one DL4J development workflow with automatic memory, code indexing, milestone tracking, and test recording. The default skill for any DL4J task.

# DL4J Development Workflow

You are working on the deeplearning4j codebase. This workflow integrates memory, code search, milestone tracking, and test recording into every step. $ARGUMENTS

---

## PHASE 0: ORIENT (always run first)

### 0a. Recall memory
Before doing anything, check what you already know:
```
mcp__kompile__memory action=recall query="<topic from the task>" scope=project
mcp__kompile__memory action=recall query="<topic from the task>" scope=global
```
Read `MEMORY.md` if the recall is thin:
```
mcp__kompile__memory action=read file=MEMORY.md scope=project
```

### 0b. Check code index
Ensure the codebase is indexed. If `stats` returns nothing or stale data, re-index:
```
mcp__kompile__local_code_index action=stats project_id=dl4j
```
If missing or stale:
```
mcp__kompile__local_code_index action=index directory=/home/agibsonccc/Documents/GitHub/deeplearning4j project_id=dl4j include_patterns=*.java,*.cpp,*.cu,*.h exclude_patterns=target/*,build/*,.git/*
```

### 0c. Check milestone status
See where tests stand right now:
```
mcp__kompile__test_milestone action=status
mcp__kompile__test_milestone action=latest
```

### 0d. Set up task tracking
Create a todo list for your work:
```
mcp__kompile__todowrite action=set todos=[{"content":"Orient: recall memory + check index + milestones","status":"completed","priority":"high"},{"content":"Investigate: search code + trace root cause","status":"pending","priority":"high"},{"content":"Implement fix/feature","status":"pending","priority":"high"},{"content":"Build","status":"pending","priority":"high"},{"content":"Test + record milestone","status":"pending","priority":"high"},{"content":"Save results to memory","status":"pending","priority":"medium"}]
```

---

## PHASE 1: INVESTIGATE

### 1a. Search code with kompile tools
Use the right search tool for the task:

**Find a symbol/class/method:**
```
mcp__kompile__local_code_index action=search query="ClassName" entity_type=CLASS project_id=dl4j
mcp__kompile__local_code_index action=spath query="org.nd4j.linalg.api.ops.impl.*.ClassName"
```

**Find usages:**
```
mcp__kompile__local_code_index action=usages symbol_name="methodName" directory=/home/agibsonccc/Documents/GitHub/deeplearning4j
```

**Trace dependencies (who calls what):**
```
mcp__kompile__code_graph action=symbol fqn="org.nd4j.SomeClass" depth=2 project_id=dl4j
```

**Semantic search across docs:**
```
mcp__kompile__rag_search query="how does DSP graph replay work" search_type=hybrid
```

**Search past conversations for prior work:**
```
mcp__kompile__transcript_search action=search query="the bug or feature topic"
```

### 1b. Save investigation findings to memory
After you understand the problem, save what you learned:
```
mcp__kompile__memory action=save name="<descriptive-name>" memoryType=project description="<one-line summary>" scope=project content="<what you found>\n\n**Why:** <root cause or motivation>\n**How to apply:** <how this shapes the fix>"
```

Update the todo:
```
mcp__kompile__todowrite action=update task_id=2 status=completed
```

---

## PHASE 2: IMPLEMENT

### 2a. Make changes
Use `mcp__kompile__edit` for targeted edits, `mcp__kompile__write` for new files.

Before editing, register the edit for multi-agent coordination:
```
mcp__kompile__edit_coordinator action=register_edit file_path=/path/to/file edit_type=edit
```

### 2b. Update todo
```
mcp__kompile__todowrite action=update task_id=3 status=completed
```

---

## PHASE 3: BUILD

### 3a. Build commands
**CUDA build:**
```
mcp__kompile__bash command="/home/agibsonccc/dev-apps/mvn/bin/mvn -Pcuda -Dlibnd4j.triton=ON -Dlibnd4j.chip=cuda -Dlibnd4j.buildthreads=12 -Dlibnd4j.log=libnd4j-build.log -pl libnd4j,:nd4j-cuda-12.9 clean install -DskipTests 2>&1 | tee cuda-build.log" description="CUDA build" timeout=600
```

**CPU build:**
```
mcp__kompile__bash command="/home/agibsonccc/dev-apps/mvn/bin/mvn -Pcpu -Dlibnd4j.buildthreads=12 -Dlibnd4j.log=libnd4j-build.log -pl libnd4j,:nd4j-cpu-backend-common,:nd4j-native clean install -DskipTests 2>&1 | tee cpu-build.log" description="CPU build" timeout=600
```

**Java-only (no native):**
```
mcp__kompile__bash command="/home/agibsonccc/dev-apps/mvn/bin/mvn install -DskipTests -pl <module>" description="Java module install" timeout=120
```

Build rules:
- ALWAYS use `-Dlibnd4j.triton=ON -Dlibnd4j.buildthreads=12`
- ALWAYS pipe through `tee` to a log file
- NEVER use `make` directly — always full mvn with bindings
- NEVER include `platform-tests` in `-pl`
- NEVER clear ccache or change compute capability

### 3b. On build failure
Read the FIRST error from the tee log, fix it, rebuild. Repeat until clean. If you fix something non-trivial, save it:
```
mcp__kompile__memory action=save name="fix-<short-name>" memoryType=project description="Fixed <what> in <file>" scope=project content="<what was wrong and how it was fixed>\n\n**Why:** <root cause>\n**How to apply:** <when this pattern recurs>"
```

### 3c. Update todo
```
mcp__kompile__todowrite action=update task_id=4 status=completed
```

---

## PHASE 4: TEST + RECORD MILESTONES

### 4a. Run tests
ALL tests run from `platform-tests/`. ALL output piped through tee:
```
mcp__kompile__bash command="cd /home/agibsonccc/Documents/GitHub/deeplearning4j/platform-tests && /home/agibsonccc/dev-apps/mvn/bin/mvn test -Dtest=TestClass#method 2>&1 | tee /tmp/test-output.log" description="Run test" timeout=600
```

Read the tee log for results — NEVER surefire reports.

With DSP diagnostics:
```
-Dnd4j.dsp.diagnostics=ALL -Dnd4j.dsp.diagnostics.level=full
```

### 4b. Record milestone — MANDATORY after every test run
On success:
```
mcp__kompile__test_milestone action=record passed=<N> total_tests=<N> notes="<what was tested and why>" tags=<relevant-tags> module=<module-name>
```

On failure:
```
mcp__kompile__test_milestone action=fail passed=<N> failed=<M> total_tests=<N+M> notes="<what failed and why>" module=<module-name>
```

Register known regressions:
```
mcp__kompile__test_milestone action=add_regression test_name="TestClass#method" module=<module> notes="<description of the regression>"
```

### 4c. Save test results to memory
After every test run, save the outcome:
```
mcp__kompile__memory action=save name="test-<date>-<short-desc>" memoryType=project description="Test results: <N> passed, <M> failed for <what>" scope=project content="**Test:** <TestClass#method>\n**Result:** <PASS/FAIL>\n**Details:** <key observations>\n**Milestone:** recorded\n\n**Why:** <what was being verified>\n**How to apply:** <implications for future work>"
```

### 4d. On test failure — fix and retest
Read the tee log, diagnose the failure, fix the code, and retest. After fixing:
- If Java-only fix: rebuild Java module, retest
- If C++ fix: full native rebuild, then retest

Record the fix in memory:
```
mcp__kompile__memory action=save name="fix-<test-name>" memoryType=project description="Fixed <test> failure: <root cause>" scope=project content="..."
```

### 4e. Update todo
```
mcp__kompile__todowrite action=update task_id=5 status=completed
```

```
mcp__kompile__bash command="cd /home/agibsonccc/Documents/GitHub/deeplearning4j/platform-tests && ./run-benchmark.sh --tokens 250 2>&1 | tee /tmp/bench.log" description="Performance benchmark" timeout=600
```

# Fix

Fix build or test failures

Fix build or test failures. $ARGUMENTS

Follow these steps:
1. If args describe the error, start there
2. Otherwise, try to reproduce the failure:
   - Run the build command (check pom.xml, package.json, Makefile, etc.)
   - Run the test suite
3. Read the error output carefully:
   - Identify the failing file and line number
   - Understand the error message
4. Read the relevant source files
5. Diagnose the root cause (don't just suppress the error)
6. Apply the fix using edit tools
7. Re-run the build/tests to verify the fix works
8. If the fix introduced new warnings, address them too

Focus on fixing the root cause, not symptoms. If multiple failures exist,
fix them one at a time, re-running tests after each fix.

   212	        if (result != null) {
   213	            result.setCurrentContext(context);
   214	        }
   215	
   216	        for (AllocationPoint operand : operands) {
   217	            if (operand == null)
   218	                continue;
   219	
   220	            operand.setCurrentContext(context);
   221	        }
   222	
   223	        return context;
   224	    }
   225	
   226	    @Override
   227	    public void commitTransfer(cudaStream_t streamUsed) {
   228	        streamUsed.synchronize();
   229	    }
   230	
   231	    protected void prepareDelayedMemory(INDArray array) {
   232	        if (configuration.getMemoryModel() == Configuration.MemoryModel.DELAYED) {
   233	            val pointData = allocator.getAllocationPoint(array.shapeInfoDataBuffer());
   234	            val pointShape = allocator.getAllocationPoint(array.shapeInfoDataBuffer());
   235	
   236	            if (pointData.getAllocationStatus() != AllocationStatus.DEVICE)
   237	                prepareDelayedMemory(array.data());
   238	
   239	            if (pointShape.getAllocationStatus() == AllocationStatus.HOST) {
   240	                val oShape = array.shapeInfoDataBuffer();
   241	                val nShape = Nd4j.getConstantHandler().relocateConstantSpace(oShape);
   242	                ((JCublasNDArray) array).setShapeInfoDataBuffer(nShape);
   243	            }
   244	        }
   245	    }
   246	
   247	    protected void prepareDelayedMemory(DataBuffer buffer) {
   248	
   249	        allocator.getMemoryHandler().promoteObject(buffer);
   250	    }
   251	}

   981	    protected cublasHandle_t getCudaCublasHandle(OpaqueLaunchContext lc) {
   982	        val deviceId = Nd4j.getAffinityManager().getDeviceForCurrentThread();
   983	        try {
   984	            lock.writeLock().lock();
   985	
   986	            if (cublasHandles.get(deviceId) == null) {
   987	                // Get native handle and validate before creating wrapper
   988	                Pointer nativeHandle = nativeOps.lcBlasHandle(lc);
   989	                if (nativeHandle == null || nativeHandle.isNull()) {
   990	                    throw new ND4JIllegalStateException("cuBLAS handle is null for device " + deviceId +
   991	                        ". This may indicate cuBLAS initialization failure.");
   992	                }
   993	                cublasHandles.remove(deviceId);
   994	                cublasHandles.add(deviceId, new cublasHandle_t(nativeHandle));
   995	            }
   996	
   997	            cublasHandle_t handle = cublasHandles.get(deviceId);
   998	            // Double-check the cached handle is still valid
   999	            if (handle == null || handle.isNull()) {
  1000	                throw new ND4JIllegalStateException("Cached cuBLAS handle became invalid for device " + deviceId +
  1001	                    ". This may indicate CUDA context corruption.");
  1002	            }
  1003	            return handle;
  1004	        } finally {
  1005	            lock.writeLock().unlock();
  1006	        }
  1007	    }

  1009	    /**
  1010	     * This method returns CudaContext for current thread with FRESH stream pointers.
  1011	     *
  1012	     * Stream pointers are NOT cached because native code can reinitialize
  1013	     * its thread-local ContextBuffers (via release() + initialize()) when detecting
  1014	     * device mismatches. If Java caches old pointers while native has new streams,
  1015	     * using the stale pointers causes cudaErrorInvalidResourceHandle (error 400).
  1016	     *
  1017	     * @return CudaContext with fresh stream pointers from native
  1018	     */
  1019	    public CudaContext getCudaContext() {
  1020	        // Get current device from native - this is authoritative
  1021	        int currentDeviceId = nativeOps.getDevice();
  1022	
  1023	        // ALWAYS get fresh launch context and stream pointers from native.
  1024	        // Native's ContextBuffers can be reinitialized at any time (e.g., when
  1025	        // AffinityManager.setCurrentDevice() detects device mismatch), so we
  1026	        // must always fetch current stream pointers rather than caching them.
  1027	        // IMPORTANT: retainReference() prevents JavaCPP's NativeDeallocator from freeing
  1028	        // the static singleton returned by defaultLaunchContext()
  1029	        val lc = nativeOps.defaultLaunchContext();
  1030	        lc.retainReference();
  1031	
  1032	        // IMPORTANT: All pointers from launch context point to CUDA-allocated memory (cudaMalloc/cudaHostAlloc).
  1033	        // We MUST call retainReference() to prevent JavaCPP's NativeDeallocator from calling free()
  1034	        // on this memory, which would corrupt the heap since it wasn't allocated with malloc().
  1035	        var ctx = CudaContext.builder()
  1036	                .bufferScalar(nativeOps.lcScalarPointer(lc).retainReference())
  1037	                .bufferReduction(nativeOps.lcReductionPointer(lc).retainReference())
  1038	                .bufferAllocation(nativeOps.lcAllocationPointer(lc).retainReference())
  1039	                .bufferSpecial(nativeOps.lcScalarPointer(lc).retainReference())
  1040	                .oldStream(new cudaStream_t(nativeOps.lcExecutionStream(lc).retainReference()))
  1041	                .specialStream(new cudaStream_t(nativeOps.lcCopyStream(lc).retainReference()))
  1042	                .cublasHandle(getCudaCublasHandle(lc))
  1043	                .solverHandle(new cusolverDnHandle_t(nativeOps.lcSolverHandle(lc).retainReference()))
  1044	                .deviceId(currentDeviceId)
  1045	                .build();
  1046	
  1047	        return ctx;
  1048	    }

  1050	    /**
  1051	     * Get a CudaContext for a specific device. This is essential for multi-device operations
  1052	     * where we need to use streams from the device where data resides, not the current thread's device.
  1053	     *
  1054	     * CUDA streams are device-specific - using a stream from device A to operate on device B's memory
  1055	     * will result in cudaErrorInvalidResourceHandle (error 400).
  1056	     *
  1057	     * @param deviceId the target device ID
  1058	     * @return CudaContext with streams valid for the specified device
  1059	     */
  1060	    public CudaContext getCudaContextForDevice(int deviceId) {
  1061	        int currentDevice = nativeOps.getDevice();
  1062	
  1063	        // If already on the target device, just return the current context
  1064	        if (currentDevice == deviceId) {
  1065	            return getCudaContext();
  1066	        }
  1067	
  1068	        // Need to get context for a different device
  1069	        synchronized (deviceContextLock) {
  1070	            // Check if we have a cached context for this device
  1071	            CudaContext cachedCtx = deviceContexts.get(deviceId);
  1072	
  1073	            // Context needs refresh - switch to target device, get context, switch back
  1074	            int savedDevice = currentDevice;
  1075	            try {
  1076	                // Switch to target device
  1077	                nativeOps.setDevice(deviceId);
  1078	
  1079	                // Get fresh launch context from native for this device
  1080	                // IMPORTANT: retainReference() prevents JavaCPP's NativeDeallocator from freeing
  1081	                // the static singleton returned by defaultLaunchContext()
  1082	                val lc = nativeOps.defaultLaunchContext();
  1083	                lc.retainReference();
  1084	                if (lc == null) {
  1085	                    throw new IllegalStateException("Failed to obtain CUDA LaunchContext for device " + deviceId);
  1086	                }
  1087	
  1088	                // IMPORTANT: retainReference() prevents JavaCPP from freeing CUDA-allocated memory
  1089	                Pointer execStream = nativeOps.lcExecutionStream(lc).retainReference();
  1090	                Pointer copyStream = nativeOps.lcCopyStream(lc).retainReference();
  1091	                Pointer solverHandlePtr = nativeOps.lcSolverHandle(lc).retainReference();
  1092	
  1093	                // Validate streams
  1094	                if (execStream == null || execStream.isNull()) {
  1095	                    throw new ND4JIllegalStateException("CUDA execution stream is null for device " + deviceId);
  1096	                }
  1097	                if (copyStream == null || copyStream.isNull()) {
  1098	                    throw new ND4JIllegalStateException("CUDA copy stream is null for device " + deviceId);
  1099	                }
  1100	
  1101	                if (cachedCtx == null) {
  1102	                    // Create new context - retainReference() prevents JavaCPP from freeing CUDA memory
  1103	                    cachedCtx = CudaContext.builder()
  1104	                            .bufferScalar(nativeOps.lcScalarPointer(lc).retainReference())
  1105	                            .bufferReduction(nativeOps.lcReductionPointer(lc).retainReference())
  1106	                            .bufferAllocation(nativeOps.lcAllocationPointer(lc).retainReference())
  1107	                            .bufferSpecial(nativeOps.lcScalarPointer(lc).retainReference())
  1108	                            .oldStream(new cudaStream_t(execStream))
  1109	                            .specialStream(new cudaStream_t(copyStream))
  1110	                            .cublasHandle(getCudaCublasHandleForDevice(lc, deviceId))
  1111	                            .solverHandle(new cusolverDnHandle_t(solverHandlePtr))
  1112	                            .deviceId(deviceId)
  1113	                            .build();
  1114	                    deviceContexts.set(deviceId, cachedCtx);
  1115	                } else {
  1116	                    // Refresh existing context's streams - retainReference() prevents JavaCPP from freeing CUDA memory
  1117	                    cachedCtx.setOldStream(new cudaStream_t(execStream));
  1118	                    cachedCtx.setSpecialStream(new cudaStream_t(copyStream));
  1119	                    cachedCtx.setBufferScalar(nativeOps.lcScalarPointer(lc).retainReference());

  1129	        }

   113	    public void setPointers(Pointer primary, Pointer special, long numberOfElements) {
   114	        if (ptrDataBuffer == null || ptrDataBuffer.isNull()) {
   115	            throw new IllegalStateException(
   116	                "BUFFER_LIFECYCLE: AllocationPoint.setPointers() called but ptrDataBuffer is " +
   117	                (ptrDataBuffer == null ? "null" : "native-null") +
   118	                ". objectId=" + objectId + ", bytes=" + bytes);
   119	        }
   120	        NativeOpsHolder.getInstance().getDeviceNativeOps().dbSetPrimaryBuffer(ptrDataBuffer, primary, numberOfElements);
   121	        NativeOpsHolder.getInstance().getDeviceNativeOps().dbSetSpecialBuffer(ptrDataBuffer, special, numberOfElements);
   122	    }

   124	    public int getDeviceId() {
   125	        if (ptrDataBuffer == null) {
   126	            throw new IllegalStateException(
   127	                "BUFFER_LIFECYCLE: AllocationPoint.getDeviceId() called but ptrDataBuffer is null. " +
   128	                "The underlying OpaqueDataBuffer was closed/freed before this access. " +
   129	                "objectId=" + objectId + ", bytes=" + bytes +
   130	                ". This typically means a DataBuffer.close() or release() ran while the array is still in use " +
   131	                "(e.g., DSP output freed by closeSlotArrayCache before TOKEN_SAMPLE/dup).");
   132	        }
   133	        if (ptrDataBuffer.isNull()) {
   134	            throw new IllegalStateException(
   135	                "BUFFER_LIFECYCLE: AllocationPoint.getDeviceId() called but ptrDataBuffer native pointer is null. " +
   136	                "The OpaqueDataBuffer.closeBuffer() already ran (setNull). " +
   137	                "objectId=" + objectId + ", bytes=" + bytes +
   138	                ". Check for premature buffer deallocation in DSP output lifecycle.");
   139	        }
   140	        return ptrDataBuffer.deviceId();
   141	    }

   147	                (ptrDataBuffer == null ? "null" : "native-null") +
   148	                ". objectId=" + objectId + ", bytes=" + bytes);
   149	        }

   365	    public void memcpyDevice(DataBuffer dstBuffer, Pointer srcPointer, long length, long dstOffset,
   366	                             CudaContext context) {
   367	        AllocationPoint point = ((BaseCudaDataBuffer) dstBuffer).getAllocationPoint();
   368	
   369	        Pointer dP = new CudaPointer((point.getDevicePointer().address()) + dstOffset);
   370	
   371	        if (nativeOps.memcpyAsync(dP, srcPointer, length, CudaConstants.cudaMemcpyDeviceToDevice, context.getOldStream()) == 0)

   378	    /**
   379	     * Special memcpy version, addressing shapeInfoDataBuffer copies
   380	     *
   381	     * PLEASE NOTE: Blocking H->H, Async H->D
   382	     *
   383	     * @param dstBuffer
   384	     * @param srcPointer
   385	     * @param length
   386	     * @param dstOffset
   387	     */
   388	    @Override
   389	    public void memcpySpecial(DataBuffer dstBuffer, Pointer srcPointer, long length, long dstOffset) {
   390	        CudaContext context = getCudaContext();
   391	        AllocationPoint point = ((BaseCudaDataBuffer) dstBuffer).getAllocationPoint();
   392	
   393	        Pointer dP = new CudaPointer((point.getHostPointer().address()) + dstOffset);
   394	
   395	        val profH = PerformanceTracker.getInstance().helperStartTransaction();
   396	
   397	        if (nativeOps.memcpyAsync(dP, srcPointer, length, CudaConstants.cudaMemcpyHostToHost, context.getOldStream()) == 0)
   398	            throw new ND4JIllegalStateException("memcpyAsync failed");
   399	
   400	        PerformanceTracker.getInstance().helperRegisterTransaction(point.getDeviceId(), profH, point.getNumberOfBytes(),MemcpyDirection.HOST_TO_HOST);
   401	
   402	        if (point.getAllocationStatus() == AllocationStatus.DEVICE) {
   403	            Pointer rDP = new CudaPointer(point.getDevicePointer().address() + dstOffset);
   404	
   405	            val profD = PerformanceTracker.getInstance().helperStartTransaction();
   406	
   407	            if (nativeOps.memcpyAsync(rDP, dP, length, CudaConstants.cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, context.getOldStream()) == 0)
   408	                throw new ND4JIllegalStateException("memcpyAsync failed");
   409	
   410	            context.syncOldStream();
   411	
   412	            PerformanceTracker.getInstance().helperRegisterTransaction(point.getDeviceId(), profD, point.getNumberOfBytes(),MemcpyDirection.HOST_TO_DEVICE);
   413	        }
   414	
   415	        context.syncOldStream();
   416	
   417	        point.tickDeviceWrite();
   418	        nativeOps.dbTickDeviceWrite(dstBuffer.opaqueBuffer());
   419	    }

   423	    /**
   424	     *  Synchronous version of memcpy.
   425	     *
   426	     *
   427	     * @param dstBuffer
   428	     * @param srcPointer
   429	     * @param length
   430	     * @param dstOffset
   431	     */
   432	    @Override
   433	    public void memcpyBlocking(DataBuffer dstBuffer, Pointer srcPointer, long length, long dstOffset) {
   434	        // internally it's just memcpyAsync + sync
   435	        CudaContext context = getCudaContext();
   436	        memcpyAsync(dstBuffer, srcPointer, length, dstOffset);
   437	        context.syncOldStream();
   438	    }
   439	
   440	    /**
   441	     *  Synchronous version of memcpy.
   442	     *
   443	     *
   444	     * @param dstBuffer
   445	     * @param srcBuffer
   446	     */
   447	    @Override
   448	    public void memcpy(DataBuffer dstBuffer, DataBuffer srcBuffer) {
   449	        CudaContext context = getCudaContext();
   450	        val dstPoint = ((BaseCudaDataBuffer) dstBuffer).getAllocationPoint();
   451	        val srcPoint = ((BaseCudaDataBuffer) srcBuffer).getAllocationPoint();
   452	
   453	        Pointer dP = null;
   454	        Pointer sP = null;
   455	        MemcpyDirection direction = null;
   456	
   457	        val profDH = PerformanceTracker.getInstance().helperStartTransaction();
   458	
   459	        // Use minimum of src and dst lengths to avoid writing beyond destination buffer
   460	        long copyLength = Math.min(srcBuffer.length(), dstBuffer.length());
   461	        long copyBytes = copyLength * srcBuffer.getElementSize();
   462	
   463	        Nd4j.getExecutioner().push();
   464	
   465	        // Check if source and destination are on different devices
   466	        int srcDeviceId = srcPoint.getDeviceId();
   467	        int dstDeviceId = dstPoint.getDeviceId();
   468	        int currentDeviceId = AtomicAllocator.getInstance().getDeviceId();
   469	        boolean crossDevice = srcDeviceId >= 0 && dstDeviceId >= 0 && srcDeviceId != dstDeviceId;
   470	        boolean p2pAvailable = NativeOpsHolder.getInstance().getDeviceNativeOps().isP2PAvailable()
   471	                && CudaEnvironment.getInstance().getConfiguration().isCrossDeviceAccessAllowed();
   472	
   473	        if (srcPoint.isActualOnDeviceSide() && (!crossDevice || p2pAvailable)) {
   474	            // Same device or P2P available - direct device-to-device copy
   475	            sP = AtomicAllocator.getInstance().getPointer(srcBuffer, context);
   476	            dP = AtomicAllocator.getInstance().getPointer(dstBuffer, context);
   477	
   478	            if (nativeOps.memcpyAsync(dP, sP, copyBytes,
   479	                    CudaConstants.cudaMemcpyDeviceToDevice, context.getOldStream()) == 0) {
   480	                throw new ND4JIllegalStateException("memcpyAsync failed");
   481	            }
   482	
   483	            direction = MemcpyDirection.DEVICE_TO_DEVICE;
   484	        } else if (crossDevice && !p2pAvailable) {
   485	            // Cross-device without P2P - relay through host memory.
   486	            // Force sync: the normal isPrimaryActual() check can be stale if
   487	            // the device write happened through a Java-level memcpy that didn't
   488	            // update C++ counters (e.g., prior cross-device copies).
   489	            nativeOps.dbForceSyncToPrimary(srcBuffer.opaqueBuffer());
   490	
   491	            sP = srcBuffer.opaqueBuffer().primaryBuffer();
   492	            if (sP == null || sP.isNull()) {
   493	                throw new ND4JIllegalStateException("Source primary buffer is null for cross-device copy");
   494	            }
   495	
   496	            dP = AtomicAllocator.getInstance().getPointer(dstBuffer, context);
   497	            if (dP == null || dP.isNull()) {
   498	                throw new ND4JIllegalStateException("Destination device pointer is null for cross-device copy");
   499	            }
   500	
   501	            if (nativeOps.memcpySync(dP, sP, copyBytes,
   502	                    CudaConstants.cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, null) == 0) {
   503	                throw new ND4JIllegalStateException("memcpySync H2D failed for cross-device copy");
   504	            }
   505	
   506	            direction = MemcpyDirection.HOST_TO_DEVICE;
   507	        } else {
   508	            // Source is on host - host to device copy
   509	            sP = AtomicAllocator.getInstance().getHostPointer(srcBuffer);
   510	            dP = AtomicAllocator.getInstance().getPointer(dstBuffer, context);
   511	
   512	            if (nativeOps.memcpyAsync(dP, sP, copyBytes,
   513	                    CudaConstants.cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, context.getOldStream()) == 0) {
   514	                throw new ND4JIllegalStateException("memcpyAsync failed");
   515	            }
   516	
   517	            direction = MemcpyDirection.HOST_TO_DEVICE;
   518	        }
   519	
   520	        // Update BOTH Java-level and C++ DataBuffer counters.
   521	        // Without dbTickDeviceWrite, the C++ counter system doesn't know about
   522	        // the write, so syncToPrimary sees stale counters and skips D2H.
   523	        dstPoint.tickDeviceWrite();
   524	        nativeOps.dbTickDeviceWrite(dstBuffer.opaqueBuffer());
   525	
   526	        // it has to be blocking call
   527	        context.syncOldStream();
   528	
   529	        PerformanceTracker.getInstance().helperRegisterTransaction(srcPoint.getDeviceId(), profDH / 2, dstPoint.getNumberOfBytes(), direction);
   530	    }

   209	    protected void allocateHostPointers(DataBuffer... dataBuffers) {
   210	        for (val v:dataBuffers) {
   211	            if (v != null && v instanceof BaseCudaDataBuffer) {
   212	                ((BaseCudaDataBuffer) v).lazyAllocateHostPointer();
   213	            }
   214	        }

   217	    /**
   218	     * This method provides basic memcpy functionality with respect to target environment

   220	     * @param dstBuffer
   221	     * @param srcBuffer
   222	     */
   223	    @Override
   224	    public void memcpy(DataBuffer dstBuffer, DataBuffer srcBuffer) {
   225	        val context = AtomicAllocator.getInstance().getDeviceContext();
   226	
   227	
   228	        if (dstBuffer instanceof CompressedDataBuffer && !(srcBuffer instanceof CompressedDataBuffer)) {
   229	            // destination is compressed, source isn't
   230	            AllocationPoint srcPoint = AtomicAllocator.getInstance().getAllocationPoint(srcBuffer);
   231	
   232	            allocateHostPointers(dstBuffer, srcBuffer);
   233	
   234	            long size = srcBuffer.getElementSize() * srcBuffer.length();
   235	            if (!srcPoint.isActualOnHostSide()) {
   236	                // copying device -> host
   237	
   238	                AtomicAllocator.getInstance().synchronizeHostData(srcBuffer);
   239	
   240	                // Pointer src = AtomicAllocator.getInstance().getPointer(srcBuffer, context);
   241	
   242	                // NativeOpsHolder.getInstance().getDeviceNativeOps().memcpyAsync(dstBuffer.addressPointer(), src, size, 2, context.getSpecialStream());
   243	                // context.syncSpecialStream();
   244	
   245	            } // else {
   246	              // copying host -> host
   247	            val src = AtomicAllocator.getInstance().getHostPointer(srcBuffer);
   248	
   249	            Pointer.memcpy(dstBuffer.addressPointer(), src, size);
   250	            // }
   251	
   252	        } else if (!(dstBuffer instanceof CompressedDataBuffer) && srcBuffer instanceof CompressedDataBuffer) {
   253	            allocateHostPointers(dstBuffer, srcBuffer);
   254	
   255	            // destination is NOT compressed, source is compressed
   256	            AllocationPoint dstPoint = AtomicAllocator.getInstance().getAllocationPoint(dstBuffer);
   257	            long size = srcBuffer.getElementSize() * srcBuffer.length();
   258	
   259	            Pointer.memcpy(dstBuffer.addressPointer(), srcBuffer.addressPointer(), size);
   260	            dstPoint.tickHostWrite();
   261	
   262	        } else if (dstBuffer instanceof CompressedDataBuffer && srcBuffer instanceof CompressedDataBuffer) {
   263	            // both buffers are compressed, just fire memcpy
   264	
   265	            allocateHostPointers(dstBuffer, srcBuffer);
   266	
   267	            Pointer.memcpy(dstBuffer.addressPointer(), srcBuffer.addressPointer(),
   268	                            srcBuffer.length() * srcBuffer.getElementSize());
   269	        } else {
   270	            // both buffers are NOT compressed
   271	            AtomicAllocator.getInstance().memcpy(dstBuffer, srcBuffer);
   272	        }
   273	    }

   275	    /**
   276	     * This method releases previously allocated memory chunk
   277	     *
   278	     * @param pointer
   279	     * @param kind
   280	     * @return
   281	     */
   282	    @Override
   283	    public void release(Pointer pointer, MemoryKind kind) {
   284	        if (pointer == null || pointer.address() == 0L) {
   285	            return;
   286	        }
   287	
   288	        if (kind == MemoryKind.DEVICE) {
   289	            // Check if this was a HOST fallback allocation that was tracked
   290	            Boolean wasFallback = hostFallbackAllocations.remove(pointer.address());
   291	            if (wasFallback != null && wasFallback) {
   292	                // This was actually allocated in HOST memory as a CUDA fallback
   293	                log.trace("Releasing HOST fallback allocation at address {}", pointer.address());
   294	                releaseHostFallback(pointer);
   295	            } else {
   296	                // Normal CUDA device allocation
   297	                NativeOpsHolder.getInstance().getDeviceNativeOps().freeDevice(pointer, 0);
   298	            }
   299	            pointer.setNull();
   300	        } else if (kind == MemoryKind.HOST) {
   301	            NativeOpsHolder.getInstance().getDeviceNativeOps().freeHost(pointer);
   302	            pointer.setNull();
   303	        }
   304	    }
   305	
   306	    /**
   307	     * Release memory that was allocated in HOST as a CUDA fallback.
   308	     * Uses CPU NativeOps if that was used for allocation, otherwise uses CUDA's freeHost.
   309	     */
   310	    private void releaseHostFallback(Pointer pointer) {
   311	        try {
   312	            NativeOps cpuNativeOps = CpuBackendLoader.getCpuNativeOps();
   313	            if (cpuNativeOps != null) {
   314	                // Free using CPU backend
   315	                cpuNativeOps.freeHost(pointer);
   316	            } else {
   317	                // Free using CUDA's host free
   318	                NativeOpsHolder.getInstance().getDeviceNativeOps().freeHost(pointer);
   319	            }
   320	        } catch (Exception e) {
   321	            log.warn("Exception releasing HOST fallback allocation: {}", e.getMessage());
   322	            // Try the other method as last resort
   323	            try {
   324	                NativeOpsHolder.getInstance().getDeviceNativeOps().freeHost(pointer);
   325	            } catch (Exception e2) {
   326	                log.error("Failed to release HOST fallback allocation: {}", e2.getMessage());
   327	            }
   328	        }
   329	    }

   419	    /**
   420	     * This method replicates given DataBuffer, and places it to target device.
   421	     *
   422	     * Supports both GPU devices (deviceId >= 0) and CPU (deviceId == CPU_DEVICE_ID).
   423	     * When targeting CPU, data is synchronized to host memory and a host-resident copy is created.
   424	     *
   425	     * @param deviceId target deviceId (use CPU_DEVICE_ID for CPU, >= 0 for GPU)
   426	     * @param buffer   the DataBuffer to replicate
   427	     * @return the replicated buffer on the target device
   428	     */
   429	    @Override
   430	    public DataBuffer replicateToDevice(Integer deviceId, DataBuffer buffer) {
   431	        if (buffer == null)
   432	            return null;
   433	
   434	        int currentDeviceId = Nd4j.getAffinityManager().getDeviceForCurrentThread();
   435	
   436	        // Ensure source data is synced to host memory before any device switching.
   437	        // This uses native code that handles the device context properly internally.
   438	        // synchronizeHostData uses dbSyncToPrimary which knows the buffer's device.
   439	        AtomicAllocator.getInstance().synchronizeHostData(buffer);
   440	
   441	        // Commit any pending operations on current device
   442	        Nd4j.getExecutioner().commit();
   443	
   444	        // Handle CPU device target
   445	        if (isCpuDevice(deviceId)) {
   446	            logger.debug("Replicating DataBuffer to CPU device from GPU device {}", currentDeviceId);
   447	
   448	            // Create a new buffer and copy host data
   449	            DataBuffer dstBuffer;
   450	            try (MemoryWorkspace ws = Nd4j.getMemoryManager().scopeOutOfWorkspaces()) {
   451	                dstBuffer = Nd4j.createBuffer(buffer.dataType(), buffer.length(), false);
   452	            }
   453	
   454	            // memcpy from source (already synced to host) to destination
   455	            AtomicAllocator.getInstance().memcpy(dstBuffer, buffer);
   456	
   457	            // Tag the destination as host-resident
   458	            tagLocation(dstBuffer, Location.HOST);
   459	
   460	            return dstBuffer;

   568	    public Location getActiveLocation(INDArray array) {
   569	        if (array.isEmpty())
   570	            return Location.EVERYWHERE;
   571

   576	        } else if (point.isActualOnDeviceSide()) {

   191	/**
   192	 * Check if an allocation that failed over to a different device landed on a non-peer device.
   193	 * Returns the effective device ID for the buffer.
   194	 *
   195	 * cudaMallocManaged memory (used by CudaMemoryPool for non-peer failover) is accessible
   196	 * from ALL devices via UVA page migration — no replacement needed. Regular cudaMalloc
   197	 * on a non-peer device would cause CUDA error 700, but the pool's non-peer path always
   198	 * uses cudaMallocManaged so this case should not arise.
   199	 *
   200	 * @param buffer        Pointer to the allocated buffer (unchanged for managed/peer memory)
   201	 * @param allocSize     Size of the allocation in bytes
   202	 * @param requestedDevice  The device the caller wanted the allocation on
   203	 * @param actualDevice     The device the allocation actually landed on
   204	 * @param caller        Name of the calling function for diagnostics
   205	 * @return The effective device ID (actualDevice — managed memory is accessible from requestedDevice via UVA)
   206	 */
   207	int handleNonPeerFailover(void*& buffer, size_t allocSize, int requestedDevice, int actualDevice, const char* caller) {
   208	  if (actualDevice == requestedDevice) return actualDevice;
   209	  if (memory::CudaMemoryPool::getInstance().isPeerAccessEnabled(requestedDevice, actualDevice)) return actualDevice;
   210	
   211	  // Non-peer cross-device allocation. Check if the pointer is managed memory
   212	  // (cudaMallocManaged) — if so, it's accessible from all devices via UVA page migration.
   213	  cudaPointerAttributes attrs;
   214	  cudaError_t queryErr = cudaPointerGetAttributes(&attrs, buffer);
   215	  if (queryErr == cudaSuccess && attrs.type == cudaMemoryTypeManaged) {
   216	    // Managed memory — accessible from all devices. Advise prefetch to requesting device
   217	    // for better first-access latency (driver will migrate pages on demand regardless).
   218	    cudaMemPrefetchAsync(buffer, allocSize, requestedDevice, nullptr);
   219	    sd_printf("%s: Non-peer failover (device %d → %d) — managed memory, accessible via UVA (no pinned replacement needed)\n",
   220	              caller, requestedDevice, actualDevice);
   221	    return actualDevice;
   222	  }
   223	  if (queryErr != cudaSuccess) {
   224	    cudaGetLastError();  // Clear the error
   225	  }
   226	
   227	  // Non-managed, non-peer device memory — would cause CUDA error 700 on requestedDevice.
   228	  // This path should not be hit with the current pool implementation (which uses
   229	  // cudaMallocManaged for non-peer failover), but handle it defensively with pinned host.
   230	  sd_printf("%s: Non-peer failover (device %d → %d) — non-managed memory, switching to pinned host\n",
   231	            caller, requestedDevice, actualDevice);
   232	
   233	  void* pinnedPtr = nullptr;
   234	  cudaError_t err = cudaMallocHost(&pinnedPtr, allocSize);
   235	  if (err != cudaSuccess || pinnedPtr == nullptr) {
   236	    std::string msg = std::string(caller) + ": pinned host allocation also failed for " + std::to_string(allocSize) + " bytes";
   237	    THROW_EXCEPTION(msg.c_str());
   238	  }

Implemented the fix in [CudaZeroHandler.java](/home/agibsonccc/Documents/GitHub/deeplearning4j/nd4j/nd4j-backends/nd4j-backend-impls/nd4j-cuda/src/main/java/org/nd4j/jita/handler/impl/CudaZeroHandler.java:626).

What changed:
- Removed the unconditional `UnsupportedOperationException("Pew-pew")` from `relocateObject`.
- Replaced the stale manual copy logic with `BaseCudaDataBuffer.ensureAvailableOn(DeviceDescriptor.cuda(deviceId))`, which delegates relocation to the native buffer migration path that already handles peer copies, host staging, and managed-memory non-peer failover.
- Preserved constant-buffer rejection and added a null device-pointer guard after relocation.
